# Guide third-party license texts

Collected from the locked dependency versions in this checkout on 2026-10-07. Includes development and optional platform dependency notices as a conservative superset. Identical texts are grouped; original copyright lines and license terms are retained. Only trailing line whitespace and terminal blank lines are normalized. This is not a claim that every listed package is linked into the Linux binary. Refresh this bundle whenever dependencies change.

The Guide upstream MIT license is distributed separately as LICENSE. Externally fetched icon catalogs are not bundled here; see THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md for optional remote resource responsibilities.

## 2. Conditions for Sharing Data

long as the Data Recipient makes available the text of this agreement
with the shared Data.

## 3. No Restrictions on Results

## 5. Definitions

or indirectly from a Data Provider and agrees to the terms of this
agreement.
